            max_context=128000,
            strengths=["локальный", "бесплатный", "приватность"],
            system_prompt=(
                "Ты — лингвист-славист. Отвечай строго в рамках "
                "академической традиции. Используй PraSlav реконструкции."
            ),
        ),
    ],
}


class FederatedLLM:
    """
    Роутер запросов между моделями по специализации.
    
    Стратегии роутинга:
    - quality: лучшая модель для задачи (по cost)
    - cost: самая дешёвая подходящая
    - capability: модель с нужными возможностями (vision, long context)
    - fallback: если основная недоступна
    """

    def __init__(self, strategy: str = "quality"):
        self.strategy = strategy
        self._last_used: Dict[str, str] = {}

    def generate(self, specialist: str, prompt: str,
                 require_vision: bool = False,
                 max_tokens: int = 2048,
                 temperature: float = 0.3) -> str:
        """Генерация с автоматическим выбором модели."""
        candidates = SPECIALISTS.get(specialist, [])
        if not candidates:
            candidates = SPECIALISTS["etymologist"]

        # фильтрация по возможностям
        if require_vision:
            candidates = [c for c in candidates if "vision" in c.strengths or
                          c.model.startswith("gpt-4")]

        if not candidates:
            raise ValueError(f"Нет подходящей модели для {specialist}")

        # выбор по стратегии
        profile = self._select(candidates)

        # выполнение
        start = time.time()
        try:
            response = litellm.completion(
                model=f"{profile.provider}/{profile.model}",
                messages=[
                    {"role": "system", "content": profile.system_prompt},
                    {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
                ],
                temperature=temperature,
                max_tokens=max_tokens,
            )
            content = response.choices[0].message.content.strip()
            latency = int((time.time() - start) * 1000)

            # логирование
            self._log_call(profile, prompt, content, latency, success=True)
            self._last_used[specialist] = f"{profile.provider}/{profile.model}"
            return content

        except Exception as e:
            # fallback
            fallback = self._fallback(candidates, profile)
            if fallback:
                try:
                    response = litellm.completion(
                        model=f"{fallback.provider}/{fallback.model}",
                        messages=[
                            {"role": "system", "content": fallback.system_prompt},
                            {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
                        ],
                        temperature=temperature,
                        max_tokens=max_tokens,
                    )
                    content = response.choices[0].message.content.strip()
                    self._log_call(fallback, prompt, content,
                                   int((time.time() - start) * 1000), success=True)
                    self._last_used[specialist] = f"{fallback.provider}/{fallback.model}"
                    return content
                except Exception as e2:
                    self._log_call(fallback, prompt, str(e2), 0, success=False)
                    return f"[LLM error after fallback: {e2}]"

            self._log_call(profile, prompt, str(e), 0, success=False)
            return f"[LLM error: {e}]"

    def _select(self, candidates: List[ModelProfile]) -> ModelProfile:
        if self.strategy == "cost":
            return min(candidates, key=lambda c: c.cost_per_1k_input)
        if self.strategy == "capability":
            return max(candidates, key=lambda c: c.max_context)
        # quality: баланс стоимости и возможностей
        return max(candidates, key=lambda c: (
            c.max_context / 1000 - c.cost_per_1k_input * 100
        ))

    def _fallback(self, candidates, exclude) -> Optional[ModelProfile]:
        for c in candidates:
            if c.model != exclude.model:
                return c
        return None

    def _log_call(self, profile, prompt, content, latency, success):
        """Асинхронное логирование в БД."""
        try:
            from app.db.postgres import SessionLocal
            from app.models.db import LLMCallRow  # type: ignore
            from sqlalchemy import text

            input_tokens = len(prompt.split()) * 1.3
            output_tokens = len(content.split()) * 1.3
            cost = (input_tokens / 1000 * profile.cost_per_1k_input +
                    output_tokens / 1000 * profile.cost_per_1k_output)

            with SessionLocal() as s:
                s.execute(text("""
                    INSERT INTO llm_calls
                        (specialist, model_provider, model_name, prompt_hash,
                         input_tokens, output_tokens, cost_usd, latency_ms, success)
                    VALUES
                        (:spec, :prov, :model, :hash,
                         :itok, :otok, :cost, :lat, :ok)
                """), {
                    "spec": profile.specialization[0],
                    "prov": profile.provider,
                    "model": profile.model,
                    "hash": hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode()).hexdigest()[:16],
                    "itok": int(input_tokens),
                    "otok": int(output_tokens),
                    "cost": cost,
                    "lat": latency,
                    "ok": success,
                })
                s.commit()
        except Exception:
            pass  # логирование не должно ломать основной поток

    def last_model_used(self, specialist: str) -> str:
        return self._last_used.get(specialist, "unknown")
"""
Temporal Graph: время бытования лексемы как измерение в Neo4j.

Модель:
(:Lexeme)-[:ATTESTED_IN {year_from, year_to, region, genre}]->(:Source)
(:Lexeme)-[:EVOLVED_INTO {year_approx}]->(:Lexeme)
(:Lexeme)-[:COEXISTED_WITH {year_from, year_to}]->(:Lexeme)
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional

from app.db.neo4j_client import Neo4jGraph


@dataclass
class TimeRange:
    year_from: Optional[int] = None
    year_to: Optional[int] = None

    def overlaps(self, other: TimeRange) -> bool:
        if self.year_from is None or other.year_from is None:
            return True
        a, b = self.year_from, self.year_to or self.year_from
        c, d = other.year_from, other.year_to or other.year_from
        return a <= d and c <= b

    def to_cypher(self) -> str:
        if self.year_from is None:
            return ""
        if self.year_to is None:
            return f"{{year: {self.year_from}}}"
        return f"{{year_from: {self.year_from}, year_to: {self.year_to}}}"


class TemporalNeo4j(Neo4jGraph):
    """Neo4j с поддержкой временных осей."""

    def add_attestation(self, lemma: str, source_code: str,
                        time_range: TimeRange,
                        region: Optional[str] = None,
                        genre: Optional[str] = None,
                        quote: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        """Добавить свидетельство бытования лексемы."""
        with self.driver.session() as s:
            s.run("""
                MATCH (l:Lexeme {lemma: $lemma})
                MATCH (src:Source {code: $source})
                MERGE (l)-[a:ATTESTED_IN]->(src)
                SET a.year_from = $yf, a.year_to = $yt,
                    a.region = $region, a.genre = $genre,
                    a.quote = $quote
            """, lemma=lemma.lower(), source=source_code,
                 yf=time_range.year_from, yt=time_range.year_to,
                 region=region, genre=genre, quote=quote)

    def add_evolution(self, from_lemma: str, to_lemma: str,
                      year_approx: int, note: str = "") -> None:
        """Связь эволюции: одна лексема → другая."""
        with self.driver.session() as s:
            s.run("""
                MATCH (a:Lexeme {lemma: $from})
                MATCH (b:Lexeme {lemma: $to})
                MERGE (a)-[e:EVOLVED_INTO {year: $year, note: $note}]->(b)
            """, from_=from_lemma.lower(), to=to_lemma.lower(),
                 year=year_approx, note=note)

    def timeline(self, lemma: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Полная временная ось лексемы."""
        with self.driver.session() as s:
            result = s.run("""
                MATCH (l:Lexeme {lemma: $lemma})
                OPTIONAL MATCH (l)-[a:ATTESTED_IN]->(src:Source)
                WITH l, collect(DISTINCT {
                    source: src.code,
                    title: src.title,
                    year_from: a.year_from,
                    year_to: a.year_to,
                    region: a.region,
                    genre: a.genre,
                    quote: a.quote
                }) AS attestations
                OPTIONAL MATCH (l)-[e:EVOLVED_INTO]->(next:Lexeme)
                WITH l, attestations, collect(DISTINCT {
                    to: next.lemma,
                    year: e.year,
                    note: e.note
                }) AS evolutions
                OPTIONAL MATCH (prev:Lexeme)-[e2:EVOLVED_INTO]->(l)
                RETURN l,
                       attestations,
                       evolutions,
                       collect(DISTINCT {
                           from: prev.lemma,
                           year: e2.year
                       }) AS predecessors
            """, lemma=lemma.lower())

            record = result.single()
            if not record:
                return {"lemma": lemma, "attestations": [], "timeline": []}

            return {
                "lemma": lemma,
                "attestations": [a for a in record["attestations"] if a["source"]],
                "evolutions": record["evolutions"],
                "predecessors": record["predecessors"],
                "timeline": self._build_timeline(
                    record["attestations"],
                    record["evolutions"],
                    record["predecessors"],
                ),
            }

    def _build_timeline(self, attestations, evolutions, predecessors) -> List[Dict]:
        """Построение хронологической ленты."""
        events = []
        for a in attestations:
            if a.get("year_from"):
                events.append({
                    "type": "attestation",
                    "year": a["year_from"],
                    "source": a["source"],
                    "region": a.get("region"),
                    "quote": a.get("quote"),
                })
        for e in evolutions:
            if e.get("year"):
                events.append({
                    "type": "evolution",
                    "year": e["year"],
                    "direction": "into",
                    "target": e["to"],
                    "note": e.get("note"),
                })
        for p in predecessors:
            if p.get("year"):
                events.append({
                    "type": "evolution",
                    "year": p["year"],
                    "direction": "from",
                    "source": p["from"],
                })
        events.sort(key=lambda e: e["year"])
        return events

    def coexistence_query(self, lemma_a: str, lemma_b: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Когда две лексемы сосуществовали?"""
        with self.driver.session() as s:
            result = s.run("""
                MATCH (a:Lexeme {lemma: $la})-[ra:ATTESTED_IN]->(src)
                MATCH (b:Lexeme {lemma: $lb})-[rb:ATTESTED_IN]->(src)
                RETURN src.code AS source,
                       ra.year_from AS a_from, ra.year_to AS a_to,
                       rb.year_from AS b_from, rb.year_to AS b_to
            """, la=lemma_a.lower(), lb=lemma_b.lower())
            overlaps = []
            for r in result:
                tr_a = TimeRange(r["a_from"], r["a_to"])
                tr_b = TimeRange(r["b_from"], r["b_to"])
                if tr_a.overlaps(tr_b):
                    overlaps.append({
                        "source": r["source"],
                        "period": f"{max(tr_a.year_from or 0, tr_b.year_from or 0)}–"
                                  f"{min(tr_a.year_to or 9999, tr_b.year_to or 9999)}",
                    })
            return {
                "lemma_a": lemma_a,
                "lemma_b": lemma_b,
                "coexistence_periods": overlaps,
            }
"""
Сервис коллаборативной разметки.
Лингвисты могут:
- верифицировать LLM-гипотезы
- оспаривать claims
- предлагать альтернативные этимологии
- рецензировать чужие аннотации
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models.db import AnnotationRow, AnnotatorRow, ClaimRow


@dataclass
class AnnotationInput:
    target_type: str          # 'claim' | 'lexeme' | 'chunk' | 'media'
    target_id: int
    annotator_id: int
    annotation_type: str      # 'verify' | 'refute' | 'comment' | 'suggest_alternative'
    content: str
    alternative_text: Optional[str] = None
    confidence: float = 1.0


class AnnotationService:
    """Бизнес-логика аннотаций."""

    def __init__(self, session: Session):
        self.s = session

    # ── создание ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    def create(self, inp: AnnotationInput) -> AnnotationRow:
        row = AnnotationRow(
            target_type=inp.target_type,
            target_id=inp.target_id,
            annotator_id=inp.annotator_id,
            annotation_type=inp.annotation_type,
            content=inp.content,
            alternative_text=inp.alternative_text,
            confidence=inp.confidence,
            status="pending",
        )
        self.s.add(row)
        self.s.commit()
        self.s.refresh(row)
        return row

    # ── рецензирование ───────────────────────────────────────
    def review(self, annotation_id: int, reviewer_id: int,
               verdict: str, comment: str = "") -> AnnotationRow:
        """verdict: 'accepted' | 'rejected'"""
        ann = self.s.get(AnnotationRow, annotation_id)
        if not ann:
            raise ValueError(f"Annotation {annotation_id} not found")
        if ann.status != "pending":
            raise ValueError(f"Already {ann.status}")

        ann.status = verdict
        ann.reviewer_id = reviewer_id
        ann.review_comment = comment
        ann.reviewed_at = datetime.utcnow()
        self.s.commit()

        # если принято — применяем
        if verdict == "accepted":
            self._apply(ann)

        return ann

    def _apply(self, ann: AnnotationRow):
        """Применение принятой аннотации."""
        if ann.target_type == "claim":
            claim = self.s.get(ClaimRow, ann.target_id)
            if not claim:
                return
            if ann.annotation_type == "refute":
                claim.status = "disputed"
                claim.confidence *= 0.5
            elif ann.annotation_type == "verify":
                claim.confidence = min(1.0, claim.confidence + 0.1)
            elif ann.annotation_type == "suggest_alternative" and ann.alternative_text:
                # создаём новый claim как альтернативу
                new_claim = ClaimRow(
                    lexeme_id=claim.lexeme_id,
                    text=ann.alternative_text,
                    status="hypothesis",
                    confidence=ann.confidence,
                    extracted_by=f"annotation:{ann.annotator_id}",
                )
                self.s.add(new_claim)
        self.s.commit()

    # ── запросы ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    def list_pending(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[AnnotationRow]:
        return (self.s.query(AnnotationRow)
                .filter(AnnotationRow.status == "pending")
                .order_by(AnnotationRow.created_at.desc())
                .limit(limit).all())

    def for_target(self, target_type: str, target_id: int) -> List[AnnotationRow]:
        return (self.s.query(AnnotationRow)
                .filter(AnnotationRow.target_type == target_type,
                        AnnotationRow.target_id == target_id)
                .order_by(AnnotationRow.created_at.desc())
                .all())

    def by_annotator(self, annotator_id: int, limit: int = 50) -> List[AnnotationRow]:
        return (self.s.query(AnnotationRow)
                .filter(AnnotationRow.annotator_id == annotator_id)
                .order_by(AnnotationRow.created_at.desc())
                .limit(limit).all())

    def stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        from sqlalchemy import func
        total = self.s.query(func.count(AnnotationRow.id)).scalar()
        by_status = dict(self.s.query(
            AnnotationRow.status, func.count(AnnotationRow.id)
        ).group_by(AnnotationRow.status).all())
        by_type = dict(self.s.query(
            AnnotationRow.annotation_type, func.count(AnnotationRow.id)
        ).group_by(AnnotationRow.annotation_type).all())
        return {
            "total": total,
            "by_status": by_status,
            "by_type": by_type,
        }
"""
Построение citation graph между академическими источниками.

Источники цитирования:
1. Ручная разметка (из предисловий, библиографии)
2. LLM-извлечение из полных текстов (по паттернам "см. ...", "ср. ...", "против: ...")
3. Структурные ссылки (Фасмер → Трубачёв → и т.д.)
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.llm.federated import FederatedLLM
from app.models.db import Source, CitationRow


@dataclass
class Citation:
    source_code: str
    cited_code: str
    page: Optional[str]
    context: str
    citation_type: str     # 'supports' | 'refutes' | 'extends' | 'mentions'
    confidence: float = 0.9
    extracted_by: str = "manual"


PATTERNS = {
    "supports": [
        r'подтвержд(?:ает|ено|ается).*?(\w+)',
        r'согласно\s+(\w+)',
        r'по\s+(\w+)\s*,',
        r'как\s+показал(?:а)?\s+(\w+)',
    ],
    "refutes": [
        r'против\s+(\w+)',
        r'в противовес\s+(\w+)',
        r'ошибоч(?:на|но|но)\s+(?:у|е)?\s+(\w+)',
        r'отвергает(?:ся)?\s+(\w+)',
    ],
    "extends": [
        r'развивая\s+(\w+)',
        r'дополняя\s+(\w+)',
        r'в продолжение\s+(\w+)',
    ],
    "mentions": [
        r'см\.\s*:\s*(\w+)',
        r'ср\.\s*:\s*(\w+)',
        r'см\.?\s+(\w+)',
        r'ср\.?\s+(\w+)',
    ],
}


class CitationGraphBuilder:
    """Построение citation graph."""

    def __init__(self, session: Session, llm: Optional[FederatedLLM] = None):
        self.s = session
        self.llm = llm
        self._source_index = self._build_index()

    def _build_index(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
        rows = self.s.query(Source).all()
        return {s.code: s.id for s in rows}

    # ── ручное добавление ────────────────────────────────────
    def add_manual(self, cit: Citation) -> CitationRow:
        src_id = self._source_index.get(cit.source_code)
        dst_id = self._source_index.get(cit.cited_code)
        if not src_id or not dst_id:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown source code: {cit.source_code} or {cit.cited_code}")

        row = CitationRow(
            source_id=src_id,
            cited_source_id=dst_id,
            page=cit.page,
            context=cit.context,
            citation_type=cit.citation_type,
            extracted_by="manual",
            confidence=cit.confidence,
        )
        self.s.add(row)
        self.s.commit()
        return row

    # ── LLM-извлечение из текста ─────────────────────────────
    def extract_from_text(self, source_code: str, text: str) -> List[CitationRow]:
        """Извлечение цитат из полного текста источника."""
        src_id = self._source_index.get(source_code)
        if not src_id:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown source: {source_code}")

        # 1. Rule-based
        rule_based = self._rule_based_extract(text, source_code)

        # 2. LLM-based (если есть)
        llm_based: List[Citation] = []
        if self.llm:
            llm_based = self._llm_extract(text, source_code)

        # 3. Merge & save
        all_cits = rule_based + llm_based
        saved = []
        for c in all_cits:
            dst_id = self._source_index.get(c.cited_code)
            if not dst_id:
                continue
            row = CitationRow(
                source_id=src_id,
                cited_source_id=dst_id,
                page=c.page,
                context=c.context[:500],
                citation_type=c.citation_type,
                extracted_by=c.extracted_by,
                confidence=c.confidence,
            )
            self.s.add(row)
            saved.append(row)
        self.s.commit()
        return saved

    def _rule_based_extract(self, text: str, source_code: str) -> List[Citation]:
        results: List[Citation] = []
        known_codes = set(self._source_index.keys())

        for cit_type, patterns in PATTERNS.items():
            for pat in patterns:
                for m in re.finditer(pat, text, re.IGNORECASE):
                    candidate = m.group(1).strip()
                    # ищем совпадение с кодом источника
                    matched_code = None
                    for code in known_codes:
                        if code.lower() in candidate.lower():
                            matched_code = code
                            break
                    if matched_code and matched_code != source_code:
                        context_start = max(0, m.start() - 100)
                        context_end = min(len(text), m.end() + 100)
                        results.append(Citation(
                            source_code=source_code,
                            cited_code=matched_code,
                            page=None,
                            context=text[context_start:context_end],
                            citation_type=cit_type,
                            confidence=0.6,
                            extracted_by="rule-based",
                        ))
        return results

    def _llm_extract(self, text: str, source_code: str) -> List[Citation]:
        known_codes = list(self._source_index.keys())
        prompt = f"""
Ты — библиограф. Проанализируй текст научной работы и извлеки все цитирования
других источников.

Код этого источника: {source_code}
Известные коды других источников: {', '.join(known_codes)}

Текст:
---
{text[:8000]}
---

Верни JSON-массив:
[
  {{
    "cited_code": "код источника",
    "page": "страница или null",
    "context": "короткий контекст цитирования",
    "citation_type": "supports|refutes|extends|mentions",
    "confidence": 0.0..1.0
  }}
]
"""
        try:
            raw = self.llm.generate("historian", prompt)
            import json, re
            m = re.search(r'\[.*\]', raw, re.DOTALL)
            if not m:
                return []
            items = json.loads(m.group(0))
            return [
                Citation(
                    source_code=source_code,
                    cited_code=item["cited_code"],
                    page=item.get("page"),
                    context=item.get("context", ""),
                    citation_type=item.get("citation_type", "mentions"),
                    confidence=float(item.get("confidence", 0.7)),
                    extracted_by=f"llm:{self.llm.last_model_used('historian')}",
                )
                for item in items
                if item.get("cited_code") in self._source_index
            ]
        except Exception:
            return []

    # ── запросы к графу ──────────────────────────────────────
    def subgraph(self, source_code: str, depth: int = 2) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Подграф цитирований вокруг источника."""
        sql = text("""
            WITH RECURSIVE cite_graph AS (
                SELECT c.source_id, c.cited_source_id, c.citation_type,
                       c.context, 1 AS depth
                FROM citations c
                JOIN sources s ON s.id = c.source_id
                WHERE s.code = :code
                UNION ALL
                SELECT c.source_id, c.cited_source_id, c.citation_type,
                       c.context, g.depth + 1
                FROM citations c
                JOIN cite_graph g ON c.source_id = g.cited_source_id
                WHERE g.depth < :depth
            )
            SELECT DISTINCT
                s1.code AS from_code, s1.title AS from_title,
                s2.code AS to_code, s2.title AS to_title,
                cg.citation_type, cg.context
            FROM cite_graph cg
            JOIN sources s1 ON s1.id = cg.source_id
            JOIN sources s2 ON s2.id = cg.cited_source_id
        """)
        rows = self.s.execute(sql, {"code": source_code, "depth": depth}).all()

        nodes = {}
        edges = []
        for r in rows:
            nodes[r.from_code] = {"id": r.from_code, "label": r.from_title, "kind": "Source"}
            nodes[r.to_code] = {"id": r.to_code, "label": r.to_title, "kind": "Source"}
            edges.append({
                "source": r.from_code,
                "target": r.to_code,
                "label": r.citation_type,
                "context": r.context,
            })
        return {"nodes": list(nodes.values()), "edges": edges}
"""
OCR для старославянской вязи и древнерусских рукописей.
Использует TrOCR (Transformer OCR) + дообучение на старославянских данных.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Optional, List, Tuple
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from PIL import Image
from transformers import TrOCRProcessor, VisionEncoderDecoderModel


@dataclass
class OCRResult:
    text: str
    confidence: float
    language: str              # 'cu' (church slavic) | 'orus' (old russian)
    boxes: List[Tuple[int, int, int, int]] = None  # bounding boxes


class SlavicOCR:
    """
    OCR-движок для старославянских текстов.
    
    Стратегия:
    1. Предобработка: бинаризация, deskew, denoise
    2. Segmentation: выделение строк
    3. TrOCR inference
    4. Постобработка: нормализация, исправление частых ошибок
    """

    def __init__(self, model_name: str = "microsoft/trocr-base-handwritten"):
        self.device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
        self.processor = TrOCRProcessor.from_pretrained(model_name)
        self.model = VisionEncoderDecoderModel.from_pretrained(model_name)
        self.model.to(self.device)
        self.model.eval()

    def recognize(self, image: Image.Image,
                  language: str = "cu") -> OCRResult:
        """Основной метод распознавания."""
        # 1. Предобработка
        processed = self._preprocess(image)

        # 2. Inference
        pixel_values = self.processor(processed, return_tensors="pt").pixel_values
        pixel_values = pixel_values.to(self.device)

        with torch.no_grad():
            generated = self.model.generate(pixel_values, max_length=512)

        text = self.processor.batch_decode(generated, skip_special_tokens=True)[0]

        # 3. Постобработка
        text = self._postprocess(text, language)
        confidence = self._estimate_confidence(text)

        return OCRResult(
            text=text,
            confidence=confidence,
            language=language,
        )

    def recognize_file(self, path: str, language: str = "cu") -> OCRResult:
        img = Image.open(path).convert("RGB")
        return self.recognize(img, language)

    def _preprocess(self, img: Image.Image) -> Image.Image:
        """Бинаризация, deskew, нормализация размера."""
        # grayscale
        img = img.convert("L")
        # binarization (Otsu)
        import numpy as np
        arr = np.array(img)
        threshold = np.mean(arr)
        arr = (arr > threshold).astype(np.uint8) * 255
        img = Image.fromarray(arr)
        # resize to 384x384 (TrOCR input)
        img = img.resize((384, 384), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        return img

    def _postprocess(self, text: str, language: str) -> str:
        """Нормализация и исправление."""
        # частые ошибки OCR на старославянском
        corrections = {
            'ѣ': 'ѣ',  # сохраняем
            'і': 'і',
            '7': 'ъ',   # частая ошибка
            '77': 'ъ',
        }
        for k, v in corrections.items():
            text = text.replace(k, v)
        return text.strip()

    def _estimate_confidence(self, text: str) -> float:
        """Оценка уверенности по длине и наличию «мусора»."""
        if not text:
            return 0.0
        # доля «нормальных» символов
        valid = sum(1 for c in text if c.isalpha() or c in 'ѣіъьѣ')
        return min(valid / max(len(text), 1), 1.0)
"""
CLIP для семантического поиска по иконам, гравюрам, миниатюрам.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import List, Tuple
from PIL import Image

import torch
from transformers import CLIPModel, CLIPProcessor


class SlavicCLIP:
    """CLIP-поиск по изображениям славянской иконографии."""

    def __init__(self, model_name: str = "openai/clip-vit-base-patch32"):
        self.device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
        self.model = CLIPModel.from_pretrained(model_name).to(self.device)
        self.processor = CLIPProcessor.from_pretrained(model_name)

    def encode_image(self, image: Image.Image) -> List[float]:
        inputs = self.processor(images=image, return_tensors="pt").to(self.device)
        with torch.no_grad():
            features = self.model.get_image_features(**inputs)
        features = features / features.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
        return features[0].cpu().tolist()

    def encode_text(self, text: str) -> List[float]:
        inputs = self.processor(text=[text], return_tensors="pt", padding=True).to(self.device)
        with torch.no_grad():
            features = self.model.get_text_features(**inputs)
        features = features / features.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
        return features[0].cpu().tolist()

    def search_by_image(self, image: Image.Image,
                        candidates: List[Tuple[int, List[float]]],
                        top_k: int = 10) -> List[Tuple[int, float]]:
        """Поиск похожих изображений."""
        q_vec = self.encode_image(image)
        return self._cosine_topk(q_vec, candidates, top_k)

    def search_by_text(self, text: str,
                       candidates: List[Tuple[int, List[float]]],
                       top_k: int = 10) -> List[Tuple[int, float]]:
        """Поиск изображений по текстовому описанию."""
        q_vec = self.encode_text(text)
        return self._cosine_topk(q_vec, candidates, top_k)

    def _cosine_topk(self, q, candidates, k):
        import numpy as np
        q = np.array(q)
        scored = []
        for cid, vec in candidates:
            v = np.array(vec)
            score = float(np.dot(q, v))
            scored.append((cid, score))
        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        return scored[:k]
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, List
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.postgres import get_session
from app.db.redis_bus import RedisBus

router = APIRouter()


class RAGQuery(BaseModel):
    question: str
    specialist: str = "etymologist"
    target_year: Optional[int] = None
    target_lemmas: Optional[List[str]] = None
    top_k: int = 8


@router.post("/query")
def rag_query(req: RAGQuery, session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    llm = FederatedLLM(strategy="quality")
    redis = RedisBus()
    pipeline = RAGPipeline(session, llm, redis)
    result = pipeline.query(
        req.question,
        specialist=req.specialist,
        target_year=req.target_year,
        target_lemmas=req.target_lemmas,
        top_k=req.top_k,
    )
    return {
        "answer": result.answer,
        "citations": result.citations,
        "retrieved_chunks": result.retrieved_chunks,
        "model_used": result.model_used,
        "specialist": result.specialist,
        "confidence": result.confidence,
    }

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, List
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.postgres import get_session
from app.annotation.service import AnnotationService, AnnotationInput

router = APIRouter()


class CreateAnnotation(BaseModel):
    target_type: str
    target_id: int
    annotator_id: int
    annotation_type: str
    content: str
    alternative_text: Optional[str] = None
    confidence: float = 1.0


class ReviewAnnotation(BaseModel):
    reviewer_id: int
    verdict: str           # 'accepted' | 'rejected'
    comment: str = ""


@router.post("/")
def create(inp: CreateAnnotation, session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    svc = AnnotationService(session)
    row = svc.create(AnnotationInput(**inp.dict()))
    return {"id": row.id, "status": row.status}


@router.post("/{ann_id}/review")
def review(ann_id: int, req: ReviewAnnotation,
           session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    svc = AnnotationService(session)
    try:
        row = svc.review(ann_id, req.reviewer_id, req.verdict, req.comment)
        return {"id": row.id, "status": row.status}
    except ValueError as e:
        raise HTTPException(400, str(e))


@router.get("/pending")
def pending(limit: int = 50, session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    svc = AnnotationService(session)
    rows = svc.list_pending(limit)
    return [_to_dict(r) for r in rows]


@router.get("/target/{target_type}/{target_id}")
def for_target(target_type: str, target_id: int,
               session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    svc = AnnotationService(session)
    rows = svc.for_target(target_type, target_id)
    return [_to_dict(r) for r in rows]


@router.get("/stats")
def stats(session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    svc = AnnotationService(session)
    return svc.stats()


def _to_dict(r):
    return {
        "id": r.id,
        "target_type": r.target_type,
        "target_id": r.target_id,
        "annotator_id": r.annotator_id,
        "annotation_type": r.annotation_type,
        "content": r.content,
        "alternative_text": r.alternative_text,
        "confidence": r.confidence,
        "status": r.status,
        "reviewer_id": r.reviewer_id,
        "review_comment": r.review_comment,
        "created_at": r.created_at.isoformat() if r.created_at else None,
        "reviewed_at": r.reviewed_at.isoformat() if r.reviewed_at else None,
    }

class ManualCitation(BaseModel):
    source_code: str
    cited_code: str
    page: Optional[str]
    context: str
    citation_type: str
    confidence: float = 0.9


@router.post("/manual")
def add_manual(cit: ManualCitation, session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    builder = CitationGraphBuilder(session)
    row = builder.add_manual(Citation(
        source_code=cit.source_code,
        cited_code=cit.cited_code,
        page=cit.page,
        context=cit.context,
        citation_type=cit.citation_type,
        confidence=cit.confidence,
    ))
    return {"id": row.id}


@router.post("/extract/{source_code}")
def extract_from_text(source_code: str, text: str,
                      session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    llm = FederatedLLM(strategy="quality")
    builder = CitationGraphBuilder(session, llm)
    rows = builder.extract_from_text(source_code, text)
    return {"extracted": len(rows), "citations": [
        {"source_id": r.source_id, "cited_id": r.cited_source_id,
         "type": r.citation_type, "by": r.extracted_by}
        for r in rows
    ]}


@router.get("/graph/{source_code}")
def citation_graph(
    source_code: str,
    depth: int = Query(2, ge=1, le=4),
    session: Session = Depends(get_session),
):
    builder = CitationGraphBuilder(session)
    return builder.subgraph(source_code, depth=depth)
from fastapi import APIRouter, UploadFile, File, Query, Form
from typing import Optional
from PIL import Image
import io

from app.multimodal.ocr_engine import SlavicOCR
from app.multimodal.clip_engine import SlavicCLIP
from app.db.postgres import SessionLocal
from app.models.db import MediaAsset

router = APIRouter()


@router.post("/ocr")
async def ocr_endpoint(
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    language: str = Form("cu"),
):
    content = await file.read()
    img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(content))
    ocr = SlavicOCR()
    result = ocr.recognize(img, language)
    return {
        "text": result.text,
        "confidence": result.confidence,
        "language": result.language,
    }


@router.post("/search/by-image")
async def search_by_image(
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    top_k: int = Form(10),
):
    content = await file.read()
    img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(content))
    clip = SlavicCLIP()

    # загрузка кандидатов из БД
    with SessionLocal() as s:
        assets = s.query(MediaAsset).filter(
            MediaAsset.clip_embedding.isnot(None)
        ).all()
        candidates = [(a.id, a.clip_embedding) for a in assets]

    results = clip.search_by_image(img, candidates, top_k=top_k)
    return {"results": [{"media_id": mid, "score": score} for mid, score in results]}


@router.get("/search/by-text")
def search_by_text(q: str = Query(...), top_k: int = Query(10)):
    clip = SlavicCLIP()
    with SessionLocal() as s:
        assets = s.query(MediaAsset).filter(
            MediaAsset.clip_embedding.isnot(None)
        ).all()
        candidates = [(a.id, a.clip_embedding) for a in assets]
    results = clip.search_by_text(q, candidates, top_k=top_k)
    return {"results": [{"media_id": mid, "score": score} for mid, score in results]}
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';

const API = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL;

export default function AnnotationWorkbench() {
  const [pending, setPending] = useState([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null);
  const [content, setContent] = useState('');
  const [verdict, setVerdict] = useState('accepted');

  useEffect(() => {
    loadPending();
  }, []);

  const loadPending = async () => {
    const res = await axios.get(`${API}/annotation/pending`);
    setPending(res.data);
  };

  const review = async (id) => {
    await axios.post(`${API}/annotation/${id}/review`, {
      reviewer_id: 1, // TODO: auth
      verdict,
      comment: content,
    });
    loadPending();
    setSelected(null);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 2fr', gap: 16 }}>
      <div style={{ background: '#161b22', padding: 16, borderRadius: 8 }}>
        <h3>Pending ({pending.length})</h3>
        {pending.map(a => (
          <div key={a.id} onClick={() => setSelected(a)}
               style={{
                 padding: 10, marginBottom: 8, cursor: 'pointer',
                 background: selected?.id === a.id ? '#1f6feb33' : '#0d1117',
                 borderRadius: 6, borderLeft: `3px solid ${typeColor(a.annotation_type)}`,
               }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.7 }}>
              {a.annotation_type} · {a.target_type}#{a.target_id}
            </div>
            <div>{a.content.slice(0, 80)}...</div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div style={{ background: '#161b22', padding: 16, borderRadius: 8 }}>
        {selected ? (
          <>
            <h3>Аннотация #{selected.id}</h3>
            <div style={{ background: '#0d1117', padding: 12, borderRadius: 6, marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <div><b>Тип:</b> {selected.annotation_type}</div>
              <div><b>Цель:</b> {selected.target_type}#{selected.target_id}</div>
              <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>{selected.content}</div>
              {selected.alternative_text && (
                <div style={{ marginTop: 8, fontStyle: 'italic', color: '#a371f7' }}>
                  Альтернатива: {selected.alternative_text}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <textarea
              value={content}
              onChange={e => setContent(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Комментарий рецензента..."
              style={{
                width: '100%', minHeight: 100, padding: 10,
                background: '#0d1117', color: '#e6edf3',
                border: '1px solid #30363d', borderRadius: 6,
              }}
            />

            <div style={{ marginTop: 12, display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
              <button onClick={() => { setVerdict('accepted'); review(selected.id); }}
                      style={{ padding: '8px 16px', background: '#238636', color: 'white',
                               border: 'none', borderRadius: 6, cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                ✓ Принять
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => { setVerdict('rejected'); review(selected.id); }}
                      style={{ padding: '8px 16px', background: '#da3633', color: 'white',
                               border: 'none', borderRadius: 6, cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                ✗ Отклонить
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <p style={{ opacity: 0.6 }}>Выберите аннотацию слева</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function typeColor(t) {
  return {
    verify: '#238636',
    refute: '#da3633',
    comment: '#1f6feb',
    suggest_alternative: '#a371f7',
  }[t] || '#8b949e';
}
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';

const API = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL;

export default function TimelineView({ lemma }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!lemma) return;
    axios.get(`${API}/temporal/${encodeURIComponent(lemma)}/timeline`)
      .then(r => setData(r.data))
      .catch(console.error);
  }, [lemma]);

  if (!data) return <div>Загрузка...</div>;

  const minYear = Math.min(...data.timeline.map(e => e.year));
  const maxYear = Math.max(...data.timeline.map(e => e.year));
  const range = maxYear - minYear || 1;

  return (
    <div style={{ background: '#161b22', padding: 16, borderRadius: 8 }}>
      <h3>Временная ось: {lemma}</h3>
      <div style={{ position: 'relative', height: 80, margin: '20px 0' }}>
        <div style={{
          position: 'absolute', top: 35, left: 0, right: 0,
          height: 2, background: '#30363d',
        }} />
        {data.timeline.map((e, i) => {
          const left = ((e.year - minYear) / range) * 100;
          return (
            <div key={i} style={{
              position: 'absolute', left: `${left}%`, top: 20,
              transform: 'translateX(-50%)',
              width: 12, height: 12, borderRadius: '50%',
              background: colorFor(e.type),
              border: '2px solid #0d1117',
            }} title={`${e.year}: ${e.type}`}>
              <div style={{
                position: 'absolute', top: 16, left: '50%',
                transform: 'translateX(-50%)',
                fontSize: 10, whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
              }}>{e.year}</div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.7 }}>
        Свидетельств: {data.attestations.length} ·
        Эволюций: {data.evolutions.length}
      </div>

      <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
        {data.timeline.map((e, i) => (
          <div key={i} style={{
            padding: 8, marginBottom: 4,
            background: '#0d1117', borderRadius: 4,
            borderLeft: `3px solid ${colorFor(e.type)}`,
            fontSize: 13,
          }}>
            <b>{e.year}</b> — {e.type}
            {e.source && <> · <i>{e.source}</i></>}
            {e.target && <> → {e.target}</>}
            {e.region && <> · {e.region}</>}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function colorFor(type) {
  return {
    attestation: '#58a6ff',
    evolution: '#a371f7',
  }[type] || '#8b949e';
}
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import cytoscape from 'cytoscape';
import axios from 'axios';

const API = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL;

export default function CitationGraph({ sourceCode }) {
  const ref = useRef(null);
  const cyRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ref.current || !sourceCode) return;

    if (!cyRef.current) {
      cyRef.current = cytoscape({
        container: ref.current,
        style: [
          { selector: 'node', style: {
              'label': 'data(label)',
              'background-color': '#d29922',
              'color': '#e6edf3',
              'font-size': 11,
              'width': 40, 'height': 40,
          }},
          { selector: 'edge', style: {
              'width': 2,
              'line-color': ele => ({
                supports: '#238636',
                refutes: '#da3633',
                extends: '#1f6feb',
                mentions: '#8b949e',
              }[ele.data('label')] || '#30363d'),
              'target-arrow-color': '#30363d',
              'target-arrow-shape': 'triangle',
              'curve-style': 'bezier',
              'label': 'data(label)',
              'font-size': 9,
              'color': '#8b949e',
          }},
        ],
        layout: { name: 'cose', animate: true },
      });
    }

    const cy = cyRef.current;
    axios.get(`${API}/citation/graph/${encodeURIComponent(sourceCode)}`)
      .then(r => {
        cy.elements().remove();
        cy.add([...r.data.nodes, ...r.data.edges]);
        cy.layout({ name: 'cose', animate: true }).run();
      })
      .catch(console.error);
  }, [sourceCode]);

  return <div ref={ref} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} />;
}

export default function ScholarDashboard() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('annotation');
  const [lemma, setLemma] = useState('волхв');
  const [source, setSource] = useState('FASMER');

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 24, maxWidth: 1400, margin: '0 auto' }}>
      <h1>Scholar Dashboard</h1>

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, marginBottom: 16 }}>
        {['annotation', 'timeline', 'citation', 'multimodal'].map(t => (
          <button key={t} onClick={() => setTab(t)}
                  style={{
                    padding: '8px 16px',
                    background: tab === t ? '#1f6feb' : '#21262d',
                    color: 'white', border: 'none', borderRadius: 6,
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                  }}>
            {t}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {tab === 'annotation' && <AnnotationWorkbench />}
      {tab === 'timeline' && (
        <>
          <input value={lemma} onChange={e => setLemma(e.target.value)}
                 placeholder="Лексема"
                 style={{ padding: 8, marginBottom: 12,
                          background: '#0d1117', color: '#e6edf3',
                          border: '1px solid #30363d', borderRadius: 6 }} />
          <TimelineView lemma={lemma} />
        </>
      )}
      {tab === 'citation' && (
        <>
          <input value={source} onChange={e => setSource(e.target.value)}
                 placeholder="Код источника"
                 style={{ padding: 8, marginBottom: 12,
                          background: '#0d1117', color: '#e6edf3',
                          border: '1px solid #30363d', borderRadius: 6 }} />
          <div style={{ height: 500, background: '#161b22',
                        borderRadius: 8, border: '1px solid #30363d' }}>
            <CitationGraph sourceCode={source} />
          </div>
        </>
      )}
      {tab === 'multimodal' && <MultimodalExplorer />}
    </div>
  );
}
# 1. Клонировать и настроить
cp .env.example .env
# прописать OPENAI_API_KEY, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY

# 2. Поднять стек
docker compose up --build

# 3. Загрузка корпусов
docker compose exec backend python -m scripts.ingest_pvl
docker compose exec backend python -m scripts.ingest_birchbark
docker compose exec backend python -m scripts.build_citation_graph

# 4. Проверка
# API:      http://localhost:8000/docs
# Neo4j:    http://localhost:7474
# MinIO:    http://localhost:9001
# Frontend: http://localhost:5173/scholar

# 5. Примеры запросов
# RAG
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/rag/query \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"question": "Когда впервые упоминаются волхвы в ПВЛ?",
       "specialist": "historian", "target_lemmas": ["волхв"]}'

# Temporal
curl "http://localhost:8000/temporal/волхв/timeline"

# OCR
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/multimodal/ocr \
  -F "file=@manuscript.jpg" -F "language=cu"

# Annotation
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/annotation/ \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"target_type": "claim", "target_id": 42,
       "annotator_id": 1, "annotation_type": "verify",
       "content": "Подтверждаю ссылкой на берестяную грамоту №210"}'

# Citation graph
curl "http://localhost:8000/citation/graph/FASMER?depth=2"
